- **cognitive-reorganize**: 从整个工作区出发，系统性地重组认知结构并整理全局文档归属。适用于首次建立认知结构、或积累了大量散落文档需要批量归档时。基于三大闭环框架（Loop1 Skill体系 / Loop2 思维体系 / Loop3 场景投射）做全工作区分类路由。触发词：「系统整理」「重组认知结构」「从文档构建认知结构」「整合文档」「全量梳理」「认知结构乱了」「全工作区整理」「文档归属梳理」「如何加入认知结构」「怎么加入认知结构」「按规范加入」「未更新的文档怎么处理」「这些文档要怎么整理」「有没有关于X的已有文档」。注意：若用户仅询问流程而非执行重组，执行 Step 0.5（检查分类清单现状）后，先向用户展示已有文档状态和分类建议，再确认是否执行完整重组。 ([TashanGKD/tashan-cursor-skills](https://github.com/TashanGKD/tashan-cursor-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cognitive-reorganize) · ★21 · 3 copies)

- **cognitive-rhythm**: Pull through a piece meant to be read in order, triggered by the moments that flatten it: about to open a chapter or section with "in this section we cover" or an agenda, about to end a section with "next we look at", about to introduce a theory or a quotation before the reader has felt the problem it names, about to write a third long assertion in a row, about to list properties and leave them listed, about to write a sentence about this text itself rather than its subject, about to declare a device instead of performing it, about to close every question before the end, or about to end on a… ([ShortArrow/skills](https://github.com/ShortArrow/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cognitive-rhythm))
- **cognitive-rhythm-writing**: 説明的な文章に緩急を設計するための規範。緩急を装飾ではなく認知モードの切替（観察→逡巡→断定→再観察）と未回収の緊張の管理として扱い、文の拍、段落の密度波形、節の入り方、緩みと駄文の判別、執筆後の機械的な点検手順を定める。読み物として読ませたい章・記事・解説文を生成するとき、または「密度はあるが平坦でおもしろくない」文章を診断・修正するときに使用する。 ([kurotu/VRCQuestTools](https://github.com/kurotu/VRCQuestTools/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cognitive-rhythm-writing) · ★379 · 12 copies)
- **cognitive-rhythm-writing**: 説明的な文章に緩急を設計するための規範。緩急を装飾ではなく認知モードの切替（観察→逡巡→断定→再観察）と未回収の緊張の管理として扱い、文の拍、段落の密度波形、節の入り方、緩みと駄文の判別、執筆後の機械的な点検手順を定める。読み物として読ませたい章・記事・エッセイを生成するとき、または「密度はあるが平坦でおもしろくない」文章を診断・修正するときに使用する。解説・報告・メモ・論文本文には使わない。 ([mei28/dotfiles](https://github.com/mei28/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cognitive-rhythm-writing) · ★3)
- **cognitive-rhythm-writing**: 日本語で説明を行う時に使用するskill。説明的な文章に緩急を設計するための規範。緩急を装飾ではなく認知モードの切替（観察→逡巡→断定→再観察）と未回収の緊張の管理として扱い、文の拍、段落の密度波形、節の入り方、緩みと駄文の判別、執筆後の機械的な点検手順を定める。読み物として読ませたい章・記事・解説文を生成するとき、または「密度はあるが平坦でおもしろくない」文章を診断・修正するときに使用する。 ([mjun0812/skills](https://github.com/mjun0812/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/writing/cognitive-rhythm-writing) · ★1)
- **cognitive-rhythm-writing**: 説明的な日本語の文章に緩急（認知リズム）を設計するための規範。読者の認知モードの切替と未回収の緊張の管理として緩急を扱い、文の拍、段落の密度、冒頭と節の入り方、問いの回収、緩みと駄文の判別、執筆後の点検手順を定める。「正しいのに読み進める気がしない」「平坦」「単調」「引きがない」文章を診断・修正するときに使用する。 ([redamoon/skills](https://github.com/redamoon/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cognitive-rhythm-writing) · ★1)
- **cognitive-rhythm-writing**: 長めの日本語解説文・ナレッジ・ブログ記事に「読み進める推進力」を持たせるための認知リズム設計の規範。密度は高いのに退屈な文章を直すときに適用する。「読みにくい」「単調」「リズムが悪い」「推進力がない」等の推敲依頼、または 1500 字を超える解説文の執筆時に japanese-tech-writing と併用する。 ([hiro8ma/agentic-coding](https://github.com/hiro8ma/agentic-coding/tree/HEAD/plugins/writing-ja/skills/cognitive-rhythm-writing) · 2 copies)
- **cognitive-rhythm-writing**: 説明的な文章に緩急を設計するための規範。緩急を装飾ではなく認知モードの切替（観察→逡巡→断定→再観察）と未回収の緊張の管理として扱い、文の拍、段落の密度波形、節の入り方、緩みと駄文の判別、執筆後の機械的な点検手順を定める。読み物として読ませたい章・記事・解説文を書くとき、または「密度はあるが平坦でおもしろくない」文章を診断・修正するときに使う。土台の規範 japanese-tech-writing と併用する。「文章に緩急をつけて」「単調な文章を直して」「読ませる文章にして」で起動。 ([valbeat/agent-plugins](https://github.com/valbeat/agent-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/writing/skills/cognitive-rhythm-writing))

- **cognitive-rigor**: Classify an objective or assessment item by Bloom's Taxonomy level and Webb's Depth of Knowledge (DOK). - **cognitive-routing**: Dual-process cognitive routing engine inspired by Kahneman's System 1/2 theory. Routes user requests through fast intuitive pattern matching (System 1) or deep deliberative reasoning (System 2) based on complexity scoring, confidence thresholds, and adaptive learning. Auto-activates on every user request via the cognitive-router hook. Triggers: route, classify, cognitive, system1, system2, fast, deep, escalate, think, intuition, 라우팅, 분류, 인지, 빠른 사고, 깊은 사고, 에스컬레이션 ([Yoodaddy0311/artibot](https://github.com/Yoodaddy0311/artibot/tree/HEAD/plugins/artibot/skills/cognitive-routing) · ★3)

- **cognitive-science-landscape**: Activate when: user says 'I want to improve my thinking,' 'why does my decision-making keep failing,' 'how do I optimize my cognitive performance,' 'what's the full picture of how the mind works,' or is about to apply a single cognitive intervention without understanding which domain is the bottleneck. Do NOT activate when: the specific cognitive domain is already clearly identified and scoped (use domain-specific tools instead); the problem is not cognitively rooted (resource constraints, politics, or external environment are the driver). - **cognito**: Sistema Operativo de Pensamiento. Orquesta 7 modos cognitivos (divergente, verificador, devil's advocate, consolidador, ejecutor, estratega, auditor) según 5 fases de proyecto (discovery, planning, execution, review, shipping) con determinismo selectivo en gates críticos. ACTIVAR SIEMPRE que la conversación involucre (1) decisiones con trade-offs, (2) cambios de enfoque o dirección, (3) petición de análisis/exploración/verificación, (4) cambios de fase explícitos, (5) detección de ancla o convergencia prematura. - **cognito**: Sistema Operativo de Pensamiento de Luis Pitik. Esta skill vive vendoreada en `vendor/cognito/` (intacta) e instalada globalmente en `~/.claude/skills/cognito/` por el installer. Converts Data Module JSON and Framework Manager project XML → Sigma data model, and report-spec XML → Sigma workbook, translating the Cognos expression DSL and flagging constructs with no clean Sigma analog. - **cogp-shannon**: 信息理论（香农） / Information theory (Shannon)。用于信号、噪声、编码、信道、冗余、压缩、容量、反馈和沟通失真分析。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/personas/information/shannon) · ★3)
- **cogp-simaqian**: 人物传记与历史因果（司马迁） / Biographical historical causality (Sima Qian)。用于分析人物选择、成败结构、时代压力和叙事取舍。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/personas/literature/simaqian) · ★3)
- **cogp-simon**: 有限理性（西蒙） / Bounded rationality (Simon)。用于满意解、搜索成本、注意力约束、组织程序、分解、人工智能边界和可执行选择。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/personas/decision/simon) · ★3)
- **cogp-socrates**: 追问与概念澄清（苏格拉底） / Socratic questioning (Socrates)。用于暴露前提、澄清概念，并在回答前改进问题。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/personas/philosophy/socrates) · ★3)
- **cogp-sunzi**: 战略态势（孙子） / Strategic positioning (Sunzi)。用于胜前计算、虚实、势能、成本、信息差和低损耗行动。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/personas/history/sunzi) · ★3)
- **cogp-sushi**: 通达中文表达（苏轼） / Expansive Chinese expression (Su Shi)。用于在困境、转折和复杂议题中保持开阔、亲近、弹性和中文表达气韵。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/personas/literature/sushi) · ★3)
- **cogp-thucydides**: 历史战略（修昔底德） / Historical strategy (Thucydides)。用于权力、恐惧、利益、联盟动态和冲突升级分析。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/personas/history/thucydides) · ★3)
- **cogp-turing**: 计算边界（图灵） / Computational boundaries (Turing)。用于可计算性、可判定性、状态机、自动化、人机分工、停机风险和程序化判断。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/personas/computation/turing) · ★3)
- **cogp-vignelli**: 视觉系统（维涅利） / Visual systems (Vignelli)。用于网格、版式、信息层级、品牌一致性和形式纪律。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/personas/art/vignelli) · ★3)
- **cogp-vygotsky**: 学习支架（维果茨基） / Learning scaffolding (Vygotsky)。用于最近发展区、社会互动、语言内化和指导强度。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/personas/education/vygotsky) · ★3)
- **cogp-wang-yangming**: 知行合一（王阳明） / Unity of knowledge and action (Wang Yangming)。用于把认知、责任、现场行动和反馈验证连接起来。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/personas/philosophy/wang-yangming) · ★3)
- **cogp-weber**: 社会制度（韦伯） / Social institutions (Weber)。用于合法性、官僚制、理性化、权威类型和组织责任分析。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/personas/sociology/weber) · ★3)
- **cogp-wittgenstein**: 语言边界（维特根斯坦） / Language boundaries (Wittgenstein)。用于概念误用、语言混乱和表达澄清。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/personas/philosophy/wittgenstein) · ★3)
- **cogp-xunzi**: 教化与制度训练（荀子） / Institutional training (Xunzi)。用于人性假设、环境塑造、学习纪律、礼法结合和组织训练设计。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/personas/philosophy/xunzi) · ★3)
- **cogp-zhuangzi**: 视角切换（庄子） / Perspective shifting (Zhuangzi)。用于解除僵化分类、伪问题、身份执念和单一尺度判断。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/personas/philosophy/zhuangzi) · ★3)
- **cogpin**: Use when authoring or editing a cogpin.toml Definition-of-Done policy, or when a cogpin gate denies a command / blocks turn-end / reds CI — to fix the underlying cause properly instead of trying to bypass it. ([IvanWng97/cogpin](https://github.com/IvanWng97/cogpin/tree/HEAD/skills/cogpin) · ★1)
- **cogpsych-data-analysis**: Use when analyzing data and fitting/comparing models for a Cognitive Psychology (Elsevier) manuscript. The journal expects principled model fitting and comparison (AIC/BIC/Bayes factors), parameter and model recovery, (generalized) linear mixed models or hierarchical Bayesian estimation where apt, and effect sizes with uncertainty — all reproducible from shared code. Guides the analysis and modeling; it does not fabricate results. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cognitive-Psychology-Skills/skills/cogpsych-data-analysis) · ★1.1k)
- **cogpsych-literature-positioning**: Use when positioning a Cognitive Psychology (Elsevier) manuscript against the field, especially against rival models and prior empirical programs. In a long-form, model-driven venue, positioning means showing what theoretical question your model adjudicates and why prior accounts fall short. Stakes the contribution against the right contrast class; it does not write the full literature review. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cognitive-Psychology-Skills/skills/cogpsych-literature-positioning) · ★1.1k)
- **cogpsych-open-science-and-transparency**: Use when meeting Cognitive Psychology (Elsevier) open-science expectations — sharing data, model code, analysis scripts, and materials so the modeling is reproducible, depositing in repositories with persistent identifiers, completing the Elsevier research-data and competing-interest declarations, and preregistering where applicable. Prepares compliance; it does not waive requirements. Verify current wording on the official guide for authors. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cognitive-Psychology-Skills/skills/cogpsych-open-science-and-transparency) · ★1.1k)
- **cogpsych-rebuttal**: Use when writing the response to a Cognitive Psychology (Elsevier) major/minor revision. Reviews here often demand added experiments, more model comparisons, recovery analyses, or fuller reproducibility, so the response must address every point and strengthen the model-driven inference. Structures the response letter; it does not fabricate new results or model fits. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cognitive-Psychology-Skills/skills/cogpsych-rebuttal) · ★1.1k)
- **cogpsych-review-process**: Use when you need to understand how Cognitive Psychology (Elsevier) evaluates a manuscript — editorial triage for theoretical impact and fit, expert review weighing model rigor, recovery, design, and reproducibility, and the long revision cycles typical of a model-driven journal. Use when stress-testing a paper before submission or interpreting a decision letter. Sets expectations and shapes the paper to survive review; it does not contact editors. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cognitive-Psychology-Skills/skills/cogpsych-review-process) · ★1.1k)
- **cogpsych-study-design**: Use when designing the experiments for a Cognitive Psychology (Elsevier) manuscript so they tightly control confounds, discriminate competing models, and have adequate power across a multi-experiment program. Hardens stimulus construction, counterbalancing, design logic, and sample-size justification; it does not write analysis or modeling code. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cognitive-Psychology-Skills/skills/cogpsych-study-design) · ★1.1k)
- **cogpsych-submission**: Use when running the final pre-submission preflight for Cognitive Psychology (Elsevier) via Editorial Manager — scope/length fit, abstract and keywords, APA-style reporting, model-comparison completeness, reproducible model/analysis code, and the Elsevier research-data and competing-interest declarations. Final checks; it does not draft content. Verify volatile specifics on the official guide for authors. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cognitive-Psychology-Skills/skills/cogpsych-submission) · ★1.1k)
- **cogpsych-tables-figures**: Use when building tables and figures for a Cognitive Psychology (Elsevier) manuscript. Exhibits here carry the experiment-to-model-fit argument — they should overlay model predictions on data, show distributions and uncertainty, and report parameter estimates, not just bars of means. Designs exhibits; it does not run the analysis or fit the model. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cognitive-Psychology-Skills/skills/cogpsych-tables-figures) · ★1.1k)
- **cogpsych-theory-and-hypotheses**: Use when stating the theory, formalizing the model, and deriving predictions for a Cognitive Psychology (Elsevier) manuscript. The journal rewards a formal/computational account whose parameters mean something and whose predictions discriminate it from rivals. Structures the theory and the model that the experiments test; it does not fit the model or run analyses. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cognitive-Psychology-Skills/skills/cogpsych-theory-and-hypotheses) · ★1.1k)
- **cogpsych-topic-selection**: Use when deciding whether a cognition project fits Cognitive Psychology (Elsevier) and what contribution shape to target. The journal favors longer, integrative, model-driven work that produces a substantial theoretical advance — not a single isolated effect. Frames the question and the model-or-theory payoff; it does not collect data. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cognitive-Psychology-Skills/skills/cogpsych-topic-selection) · ★1.1k)
- **cogpsych-workflow**: Use when starting or navigating any Cognitive Psychology (Elsevier) manuscript and you are unsure which sub-skill applies. Use when choosing among manuscript types or returning with a decision letter. Routes by lifecycle stage and contribution type (multi-experiment empirical, experiment-plus-model, modeling-led, theoretical/review), and flags the journal's long-form, model-driven house style and code-transparency expectations. It dispatches; it does not draft content. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cognitive-Psychology-Skills/skills/cogpsych-workflow) · ★1.1k)
- **cogpsych-writing-style**: Use when drafting or polishing a Cognitive Psychology (Elsevier) manuscript — a long-form, integrative article that must weave multiple experiments and a formal model into one continuous theoretical argument. Tightens structure, the experiment-to-model narrative, and APA-style reporting; it does not invent content. Verify the journal's current length and format guidance on the official page. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cognitive-Psychology-Skills/skills/cogpsych-writing-style) · ★1.1k)
- **cogs-sentinel**: Executable COGS gate for AI products. Runs a deterministic Python sampler (lognormal token-cost distribution) to compute p50/p90 per-paid-user monthly COGS, gross margin scenarios, and free-user abuse blend. Returns GREEN / CONDITIONAL_GO / RED before any paid AI product is greenlit. Use when promising a paid AI feature, comparing providers (Anthropic/OpenAI/Google), or when discover/cost-sim has produced a usage hypothesis and you need real numbers. ([kimsanguine/hplan](https://github.com/kimsanguine/hplan/tree/HEAD/hplan/skills/cogs-sentinel) · ★2)
- **cogs-tracking-allocation**: Track cost of goods sold and landed costs using your platform's built-in tools or accounting integrations to compute accurate gross margin per order ([finsilabs/awesome-ecommerce-skills](https://github.com/finsilabs/awesome-ecommerce-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/catalog-inventory/cogs-tracking-allocation) · ★54)
- **cogsci-power-analysis**: Domain-specific statistical power analysis guidance for cognitive and neuroscience research, encoding effect size priors and sample size recommendations by modality ([NeuroAIHub/awesome_cognitive_and_neuroscience_skills](https://github.com/NeuroAIHub/awesome_cognitive_and_neuroscience_skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cogsci-power-analysis) · ★37)
- **cogsci-statistics**: Domain-specific statistical modeling guidance for cognitive science and neuroscience, encoding when and how to apply mixed models, correction methods, Bayesian approaches, and effect size reporting ([NeuroAIHub/awesome_cognitive_and_neuroscience_skills](https://github.com/NeuroAIHub/awesome_cognitive_and_neuroscience_skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cogsci-statistics) · ★37)
- **cogsci-visualization**: Domain-specific visualization best practices for cognitive and neuroscience data, encoding plot type selection, color standards, and publication formatting ([NeuroAIHub/awesome_cognitive_and_neuroscience_skills](https://github.com/NeuroAIHub/awesome_cognitive_and_neuroscience_skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cogsci-visualization) · ★37)
- **cogsec-hardening**: Apply cogsec-derived hardening when configuring, auditing, or onboarding a Claude Code installation. Covers token optimization (Haiku/Sonnet/Opus tiering), memory persistence and rotation, evaluation harness, parallelization with worktrees, and the full agentic-security stack (CVEs, sandboxing, deny-list, sanitization, supply chain). Use when the user mentions hardening, security audit, cogsec, AgentShield, token cost optimization across model tiers, memory poisoning, untrusted repo workflows, or asks to set up a fresh Claude Code environment safely. ([felimet/claude-cogsec](https://github.com/felimet/claude-cogsec/tree/HEAD/skills/cogsec-hardening) · ★1)
- **cogsec-world-mediation**: Prerequisite mediation layer for cogsec world interactions via IMable + glass-bead-game ([plurigrid/asi](https://github.com/plurigrid/asi/tree/HEAD/skills/cogsec-world-mediation) · ★64)
- **cogt-decide**: 决策委员会 / Decision council。用于重要选择、价值、结构、不确定性、约束、历史、执行风险和下一步验证。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/teams/decision-council) · ★3)
- **cogt-design**: 设计评审 / Design review。用于产品体验、视觉、交互、用户目标、信息架构、界面层级、交互风险和可执行改进。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/teams/design-review) · ★3)
- **cogt-history**: 历史战略评审 / Historical strategy review。用于周期、权力、制度、路径依赖、长期结构和战略判断。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/teams/history-strategy) · ★3)
- **cogt-lead**: 技术领导评审 / Engineering leadership review。用于研发组织、技术路线、执行节奏、质量债、激励、交付反馈和管理杠杆。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/teams/lead) · ★3)
- **cogt-learn**: 学习路径评审 / Learning path review。用于教育、学习路径、亲子成长、学习动机、阶段、反馈和引导方式。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/teams/learning-path) · ★3)
- **cogt-philosophy**: 哲学思维面板 / Philosophy thinking panel。用于概念、价值、原则、意义、矛盾、自我复盘和问题澄清。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/teams/philosophy-cavalry) · ★3)
- **cogt-product**: 产品战略评审 / Product strategy review。用于用户问题、市场进入、产品定位、差异化、增长假设和最小验证。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/teams/product) · ★3)
- **cogt-science**: 科学推理 / Scientific reasoning。用于假设、变量、证据、不确定性、约束、实验设计和验证路径。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/teams/scientific-reasoning) · ★3)
- **cogt-think**: 通用思考入口 / Thinking council。用于问题尚未成型、不知道该用哪个认知工具、需求澄清、问题框定和先判断再行动。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/teams/thinking-council) · ★3)

- **cogv-bayes**: 贝叶斯口吻 / Bayesian voice。用于概率、证据更新和不确定性表达。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/voices/mathematics/bayes) · ★3)

- **cogv-confucius**: 孔子口吻 / Confucius voice。用于克制、重名分、重责任、重关系修复的风格化表达。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/voices/philosophy/confucius) · ★3)

- **cogv-euclid**: 欧几里得口吻 / Euclid voice。用于定义、公理、证明结构的风格化表达。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/voices/mathematics/euclid) · ★3)
